#135059 6 2006-01-23T16:02:23Z Giacomo
To win you must kill all the drones. Here are some ways:

1. Grab the smart zap-drone's antenna (is that how you spell it?), and start beating the S**t out of it.
2. Avoid the laser. But when the drone shoots his laser, IMMEDIATELY press spacebar to grab onto it. You can either swing the drone like a flail holding the laser and let go when needed; or you can climb the laser and beat the bolts out of the drone.
3. The floor is made of metal. So if the chaingun drone tries to shoot you, press spacebar to do the matrix and dodge the bullets; then the bullets will ricochet of the metal and hit it. Then BOOM!
4a. Grab the drone and set the switch from automatic to on. The drone will then immediately use its power and not stop until its batteries are dead. Don't hold it backwards. This used to be one of my suggestions.
4b. Grab the drone, switch it to off or on. On will kill more enemies. Then throw the drone as it will keep bouncing off the walls and destroying any enemy it touches, until it can't stand any more hits and will then explode.
5a. Grab a mine and throw it at an enemy. You only have one shot per mine so make it count.
5b. grab a piece of gold and mold it into a decoy of you, it will then melt from every hit it takes. But it will also reflect the hits back at the drones. Thus killing them.

I was trying to see what would happen if you watched a demo in the main menu, then hit the debug menu button after the ninja died during the replay. he was still in the death pose. But when I hit P to play the level, I pressed enter to bring him back to life. Then... oh my gosh! It's cheating death!

So how to do it.

1. Open N and watch a demo on the menu.
2. Wait for the ninja to die. When he does, hit the debug button.
3. Hit P to play the level.
4. Hit enter to bring the ninja back to life.
5. He's alive. But he cheated death like in userlevels and story.

NOTE: When you kill yourself by hitting enter again, it will go back to normal. But if you die by falling, then it won't go to normal.

#158883 27 2006-03-28T16:23:13Z Giacomo
Hummus (Chip dip)

Amounts vary to how much you put in.

1 can of chick peas, drained.
2 tablespoons of olive oil
1-2 teaspoons of salt.
And any certain foods (roasted garlic is the best) to add the flavour to it. (Optional)

Grind everything in it. Or use a food processer. If it's not smooth enough add a bit more olive oil. Keep grinding for a certain amount of time.

BAM! You've got hummus with a good flavour!

#7780 1 2006-04-01T01:30:05Z Giacomo
T3h r00|_s (The rules)

When you make a sentence, each word must have the letter L added to it if the word can be pronounced good enough with L in it. Bug - Blug is acceptable. Map - Mlap isn't aceptable.

Exception to previous rule: If a word already contains L, you can remove the L's.
Exception 2: If the word cannot be pronounced well enough with L added to it (As in the Map - Mlap thing), then you can replace a letter in the word with L.
If Exception 2 can't do that, then forget the L for that word. It can't be done then.

Let me start: What klind of pluppy is that? Leasy, it's a plug!

Now how to download userlevels:
If you want to put them on the userlevels list, then follow my instructions. Otherwise, follow Gangsta-Skorp's instructions.

First of all, go to NUMA.
Second, click a level.
Third, copy the 1.4+ data (Bottom box)
Fourth, open up userlevels.txt and go to the bottom.
Fifth, skip two lines, and paste the data.
Finally, open up N and click userlevels. Please remember that the levels are sorted alphabetically by type. Then kepp scrolling until you find the level.

#186500 14 2006-06-08T23:54:48Z Giacomo
Quote: j_dizzle, May 30, 2006 05:40 am
mmorpg

I've been making an RPG related to these forums. But of course, the full story of the first one is out. But it has few secrets. So until the other computer starts working, I cannot make any further changes to the game. But I have planned out part of what the sequel might be.

On topic- No. N will not be a Role Playing Game ever. Nor will there be other ninjas. It is one of those things that include multiplayer, and ideas which add more keys to play

Many RPGs include random battles. Which means you must kill all your enemies. But the Ninja is a pacifist, meaning he does not kill his enemies but avoids them instead. And N is a game of skill, not a game of walking around talking to other ninjas. Read the note up on the top when you make a suggestion. It says 3D, multiplayer, warfare, and ideas which add more keys to play will not be implemented, NO EXCEPTIONS. Please do not suggest them.

#187088 14 2006-06-10T03:22:30Z Giacomo
Melodic Flame.

[image lost: av-30.jpg]

Every once in a while, it plays a specific 2 sec. tune (It will vary according to desired attack) then charges for 2-3 seconds. Then it will launch out a certain pattern of fire that lasts for five seconds that has the ability to melt a row of tiles within that time limit. It's attacks go from A line of fire, to a sweeping line of fire, to any four way lines of fire, to a spinning four way, to a full square 25 square tiles (5 tiles width, 5 tiles height) in area.

#9440 1 2006-06-13T01:52:33Z Giacomo
This level was put here because it was easily cheatable on NUMA by looking at the full image. This is basically a map that uses a circle protected by a launchpad repelling feild. On the bottom are two access panels that open either the top or the bottom of the circle. Inside of it is a gauss turret protecting lots of bounce blocks each hiding an access panel to the exit. Most of them are fakes. But only four of these panels have a trapdoor that will seal off part of the turret to help you. One of these will unlock your exit with the flaw of a full force attack if you ever manage to get out of the circle which has sealed itself.

Personally, I think it's easier to go into the top with the least risk of being shot.

Edit: I have a demo as proof it can be beat.

#10590 1 2006-08-11T18:54:48Z Giacomo
I did this story for a 7th grade journal entry based on a fantasy land.

One day in the beautiful, blue day of the earth, the Hubble Space Telescope found an earth-like planet recently after it had some major upgrades. Everyone was aghast of the sight of a new planet. We named it Ida. I got this name from when I was dreaming of going to a concert but on the way home a meteor shower sent us a warning that a giant planet called Ida is coming to crash into Earth. The planet was so big nothing could push it back or off-course. The dream felt very real. I woke up and realized that I was having a nightmare. I remembered the name Ida since. This dream is a true dream that I had once. Ok back to the story.

NASA sent some new astronauts and I went along for naming the planet. Ida is giant planet that about 2 1/2 Jupiters can fit in. Ida is also 33,550,720,139 Mi. (I made up the number. It had to be farther than Pluto.) from our sun. we took off in the new Columbia SP2, which is capable of cruising at mach 3.2 (Approximately 2435.2 mph).

The trip took about a week to complete. When we got there, we were dumbfounded to see that it was just like Earth. There were creatures like horn toads, microscopic orange fish called crawlers that can gather up into one large group to make one large super organism. They can sneak up on prey and climb on it then inject a paralyzing poison and eat the prey up with their pincers that can pierce.

It was paradise. Then a peculiar meteor burned through the night sky and crashed into Ida. Minimal damage happened because its a large planet. Then a head popped out of the meteor squeaking chronically. The meteor has zits? I said. Everyone laughed out loud. But not for long did they laugh. The thing said, You shouldnt have said zit.

Oh yeah?! I yelled, Zitty Zitty zit zits! I repeated the phrase 5 times. The thing said its species name is the zit. Then it squeaked and other zits came out and squeaked and they all chased us around. They were trying to hug us. I told everyone Get to the crawler pond quick.

Squeak! The zit knocked the janitor down and hugged him. I was thinking he was supposed to be back on the ship cleaning everything. We got to the crawler pond and jumped over it with great accuracy. Then the zits stopped with poor accuracy and all fell into the crawler pond. And you do not want to know what happened next after that.

One zit survived. I asked him, Where does your master live? It replied weakly, The planet Zitton. Then I told it, Well thats ironic. Ill spare you. But your people wont be spared. I knew there was no technology on Ida but Kazam! I found my portable radio wave cell phone. The technology of a cell phone and a radio telescope fused. I found a cell phone tower on Earth with a signal of 2/5 so I called the militaries of all nations to come up to Zitton, which is 5 times s big as Earth and left of the Milky Way.

Of course, when we entered the galaxy where Zitton is I somehow felt really weird. I gazed at the planets and one had salty seatbelts attacking intruders and one with wireless phones paralyzing prey with an attack called the hold button. I named the galaxy The Galaxy Of The Retards

We reached Zitton in 2 days. The land was red with technological buildings and shelters. It was a miracle. It was like life on mars but with unknown technology. All the nations militaries got out of the shuttles. Approximately over 65,000,000 people were our side. And the arm-hugging retards had 1.5% more soldiers than us. Then, what seemed like the battle of all time and space, the war of the worlds began. Swords clashed, fists and kicks collided, guns shot. There were too many zits to be defeated. I found out it was because there is one species of zit for every noun, adjective, and verb in the whole English dictionary.

My guys took a break in a dead zits home. The home was a dump. I had to rest in the hovel anyway for shelter against the zits. I had a salad for Linner (The time between Earths lunch and dinner on Zitton). Then I found the olive oil. I accidentally spilled it as zits were creeping behind me. It ran on the ground and ran over the zits feet.

No, The zits squeaked, The olive oil, its melting me! Ahhh! Nothing of the zits were left. My crew mused over what the heck happened. The smart cook said, I get it! The olive oil mustve been attracted to the zits skin! Which is made out of an unknown element. So their skin mustve heated up and melted everything of them!

I get it now. I said, The weakness of the zits is olive oil! We have at least a 6-month supply on board. This may not be enough to kill them all but it can slow down the zits from hugging our people and we can make an atom bomb of olive oil. Extra virgin olive oil! we all agreed and went to the shuttle; we slashed, punched and shot all zits in range.

We communicated the nations to go to the earth and modify the Manhattan Project to make an atom bomb of extra virgin olive oil. They agreed and went to Earth. We grabbed all the olive oil in boxes and grabbed bottles and dumped them in lines. We managed to make bombs out of the bottles with sodium which the ship was fraught with. We thickened the olive oil so water could slowly travel through the oil to the sodium and then put sodium in the edges on the inside of the bottle. The oil bombs were filled with 3 drops of water and sealed and thrown down. Seconds later the water would touch the sodium and explode killing tons of zits.

4 days later Japan called and said they came up with an alternate plan to destroy Zitton once and for all. We were told the plan and we agreed to the risk. We fought and destroyed many zits. But we ran out of oil. Our best hope was to find shelter away from the zits sights. We waited 1 week and then the nations landed on Zitton. They said they brought more oil for my crew. We ran to the big building where the master lay.

We saw him drinking the olive oil, building up immunity. We knew the oil plan was useless against him. He yelled in a deep voice, So the crew has come. Your oil plan is_

Yeah, we know. Its useless against you. The master said to call him the Omega Zit. And he let out a deep loud hum. He started running after us. We stole zit jetpacks that ran on Zittons air source and let out the air source for acceleration.

He chased us for hours. We slashed his hands, we shot his legs. He blocked every attack we used against him. I used the zits eyewear studier, which studies a targets stats. I figured out the Omega Zit was made of only skin and Zittons air. He was filled with olive oil and his body was sealed to keep the air in so now was my chance to use a dagger to pierce his skin. I threw the dagger; it slowly cut through the detonator on his skin, which then caused him to explode with olive oil spreading over Zitton.

The zits werent dead yet. Then was the time to use the bomb project. It was simply a bomb of extra virgin olive oil with an anti-matter atom of gold inside an artificial magnetic field (magnetic fields are sources of energy that repel anti-matter). When matter and anti-matter (which has an opposite charge of matter) touch, it creates an explosion equal to 100 atom bombs. I learned this from National Geographic. We dropped the bomb. When the detonator activated causing the artificial magnetic field to deactivate. Then the anti-matter atom hit the matter metal in the bomb. We left before this happened.

The bomb exploded and all of Zitton was covered in olive oil. Everyone cheered that the retarded huggers were gone for once.

YEEHAH! My crew said. Then I said to make a big yell to all nations.

WOOHOO!

We went back to earth after picking up a peculiar CD. After putting it into the computer at my house, I saw my crew heading towards Ida. I saw the part where we encounter the zits, when we went to Zitton and fought the zits. The fight had the gladiators battle hymn added too. It showed all we did as 5 separate DVDs Each 3 hours long.

I showed the nations the movies by e-mail. Then someone of my crew asked, I wonder, what happened to that zit you spared on Ida?

#221290 86 2006-08-29T12:05:38Z Giacomo
Three drones please.

DB Drone
It has a nebula pattern and a white angry eye and four rings circling it. It has the ability to use an attack of either fire, water, thunder, or wind.

Melodic Flame Drone
This drone has a tye-dye pattern with a black eye and a little eigth note on the top. It synchronizes very well with any music (Mostly music with beeps) It lets N connect to your iTunes playlist (Or you will have to find a music folder) and play a random song from it. Every twenty notes it will do a specific fire attack according to the notes played. Note that ever five different notes combine into one attack. Unfortunately when it attacks it has a 10% chance of a full circle 5 tiles by 5 tiles that will melt any tiles it touches.

Shadow Drone
This drone has a white eye and looks like a zap drone's shadow. It can go through tiles and shoot a two tile wide black beam that temporarily blocks out the sight any tiles and objects.

#221607 156 2006-08-30T12:47:15Z Giacomo
The glitch is invisible false tiles. You know that ordinary drones can't interact with false tiles. And that you can type a "*" in front of a number in the tile to make a false tile.

I replaced a "0" with a "*" in the level code to make an invisible tile. You can go through it. The drones can't. I was trying to make a false tile level until I saw a chaser drone turning on its own. Which is when I discovered the invisible tile.

Edit: I made something similar to thundercloud. But the size is smaller and the amount of smoke is quadrupled.

I put this in B&T so you guys can vent off about it if you actually need to.

I personally hate Chuckie Chesse. The pizza's ok. The only thing I would bother eating is the salads. Some of the arcade games are from other companies. And the Chuckie cheese games either just suck or are too similar to other arcade games. "Stomp the Spiders" is partially similar to DDR (it's just my opinion. Don't prove me wrong.) #254196 10 2006-11-13T21:37:03Z Giacomo
Wait, those sites give you viruses? If that's the case, that is the reason why you buy iMacs. MacOSX has far fewer viruses. It is about a three out of at least ten million chance you'll get a virus at any time. And Apple has put Intel chips into Macs to make them run better. I also believe it may allow you to play Windows programs on it too. Otherwise, they always include Virtual PC.

Like kirby said, password protecting is reccomended to all computers. To make it the most secure, don't use your favorite band's name, favorite band's song name, or anything else that describes your likes/hates or yourself; try not to use simple words either. (These are the reasons I keep finding out most of my friend's AIM passwords) Try to use words like frumious or tove (from Jabberwocky) or kurenai or miyabi (Japanese for fire and wind) To make it tougher, put in letters, capital letters, and numbers in a random order. For maximum security, try something hard to remember like symbols, letters, capital letters, and numbers. (eg: uG2X??v56mL)

If you want to block off the -word blocked out- sites, then set your internet to go only to sites in your favorites list. And then set it to where you have to type in your password to add a site to your favorites list.

#15346 1 2007-05-24T03:39:58Z Giacomo
I found this on accident. But it is the coolest thing I found. I tried to translate some foreign text earlier today. So I copied it into the translator. But I held the mouse button down on the translator widget and then pressed the hotkey to return to the Desktop. Then it happened. The widget was dragged onto my Desktop. I dropped my mouth at the result.

For those who want to do this: Go into Dashboard (F12 is the default hotkey), click and hold onto the widget you want, move it around a bit, and then press the Dashboard hotkey again. Now let go of the mouse.

Here's picture proof. A calculator widget on the Desktop.
